# Run E red-team verification — round 1

Status: **INCOMPLETE_BUDGET**. Pinned HEAD: `890eea76c8c04b8d2acfcc73a895ec3112560e38`; fork and merge-base: `30896acbcb2ee127131504e531f230fcfc7e4409`.

15 meaningful mutants killed in exactly 30 supervised launches. Every baseline and mutant executed exactly one selected test; all mutant failures were assertion mismatches. No provider or database calls were needed for these unit measurements. No P0 survivor, infrastructure failure or oracle failure was observed in the measured cases. The unmeasured cases prevent a CLEAR verdict.

## Inventory and budget

The final census retains 2,110 expanded test-case identities, plus three explicit production/migration exclusion records. 722 test cases are in scope: 15 killed and 707 skipped-budget. There are 1,391 exclusions total. Measuring the remaining cases at this same HEAD needs at least 1,414 additional supervised launches. Fixes or additional mutants can increase that requirement. The generic second-round cap is 30; no Run C/D or other authorization applies.

Census chronology: static discovery first produced 2,017 rows with unresolved parameter tables. Expansion produced 2,110 test cases across 143 files. Temporarily removing 292 cases selected only through runtime-unchanged type modules yielded 1,818 cases across 131 files; that intermediate figure was communicated as locked too early. All 292 identities were restored as exclusions, preserving the broad census. No measured identity changed. Hunk/declaration and case reachability refinement reduced semantic obligations to 722. The earlier 1,096-case/2,192-launch figure was an upper bound based on whole modules, not the final budget.

Direction A uses changed individual test declarations plus reachable changed fixtures/shared hooks. Direction B traces changed runtime declarations and their local callers through named runtime imports, including dynamic imports, to individual test bodies and helper closures. Type-only imports and the two added comments in schema-ready.ts do not create runtime obligations. Static reachability identifies covering candidates; it does not prove a specific conditional branch executes. Branch execution and oracle strength remain the purpose of the unperformed measurements. Each row below retains its identity and scope evidence. Unchanged tests importing a type alone are excluded. The solicitation approval, migration096, response-generation persist and refusal writer suites each retain their census rows with zero in-scope cases.

Discovery scripts: `/private/tmp/run-e-inventory.cjs`, `/private/tmp/run-e-refine.cjs`; complete source-derived metadata: `/private/tmp/run-e-inventory-refined.json`. These are scratch census evidence, not repository verifier changes.

## Findings

| ID | Severity | Location | Finding | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E-RT-P1-1 | P1 | src/lib/agents/operator/__tests__/refusal-completion.test.ts:34 | Distinct-subject test also requires undocumented map insertion order | open |
| E-RT-P1-2 | P1 | docs/superpowers/plans/codex-owner-operator-run-e-plan.md:35 | Plan source citations no longer identify the stated completion and Apply seams | open |
| E-RT-P1-3 | P1 | src/lib/agents/operator/restore.ts:170 | New Restore preparation invariant has a Sentry emitter without an alert rule | open |

### E-RT-P1-1

The new test compares Object.keys(refusalIds) with an ordered array. The locked contract requires distinct durable subject IDs and correct reason-to-subject/ledger mapping, not object-key iteration order. A valid implementation that accumulates the same two identities in another order fails this assertion. Replace the ordered comparison with set membership or sorted membership; retain the distinct UUID and exact ledger identity assertions below it.

### E-RT-P1-2

The plan cites turns.ts:483 as fenced completion, but at pinned HEAD that line is an interface field; completeTurnFenced starts at src/lib/agents/operator/turns.ts:572. The neighboring Apply citations (292,412,476) now identify PolicyPlan, confirmation checking and writerRefusalResult rather than the collectively stated lock/writer/receipt seams. Refresh these current precedent citations with fully qualified paths and current symbols/lines. The underlying described behavior exists; this is stale navigation, not a false implementation claim.

### E-RT-P1-3

The new post-insert invariant throws "agents/operator: prepared Restore vanished in its transaction". The button route catches it at src/app/api/operator/proposals/[id]/restore/route.ts:38-40, emits reportCaughtError with endpoint api/operator/proposals/[id]/restore POST, and returns restore_failed 500. beta-launch-alerting.md contains no rule for this endpoint/invariant: its Operator Next.js rule at line422 explicitly filters the apply POST endpoint and retained-veto text; rule11 filters worker_job/agent_turn, which cannot match the button request. Add a matching operator-actionable invariant rule with identity, filter, error level, exception grouping, every-event cadence/throttle and destination action. This static finding does not claim a staging delivery drill occurred.

## Other duties and evidence

Fragile-assertion scan examined the changed Vitest/React tests for absence-of-tag proxies, exact output string counts and order assertions. Heading count checks in RestraintReport express one group per stored category; completion request ordering expresses the required business-before-account lock order. Neither was mislabeled as incidental. The map-key order assertion above has no such contract.

Docs: ran the available prevalidate-plan.py against the changed plan, local evidence and deviations. Local evidence and deviations passed. The plan produced one unresolved shorthand auto-approval/settings.ts citation; hand resolution found src/lib/auto-approval/settings.ts. All explicit plan path/line references were then compared with pinned HEAD: stale completion/Apply navigation is recorded above. Historical red/green observations and explicitly pending browser/deployment claims were not reclassified as current gate claims. The supplied full-gate log ends GREEN at the supplied HEAD; gate-fingerprint.mjs check independently returned 032ea19aff8d9915e94c6c16fbab2cc037c2551bf344a67aa00c07baead3f9bd.

Sibling search: rg patterns `RestoreEnvelope|deriveRestoreInput|restore_last|refusal_ref|operator_content_refused|operator_policy_refused` across src/lib/agents/operator, worker/jobs/agent-turn and src/app/api/operator, excluding __tests__. This covered both Restore entry paths, Apply/read provenance resolution, the worker model-index reservation, semantic duplicate refusal checks and fenced materialization. No additional missed sibling defect was established by that search. It did not audit unrelated historical code.

Operational comparison: inspected new throw/catch/reportCaughtError paths and beta-launch-alerting.md. Fenced worker failures continue through the existing worker_job + agent_turn retry-exhaustion signal, exception/error grouping, every-event cadence and Discord destination; no new worker terminal reason is added. The button Restore invariant is a new Next.js signal with no matching rule, as recorded above. This is a static six-facet comparison; no staging delivery is claimed.

## Isolation and restoration

Before mutation: pinned committed HEAD, tracked tree clean, watcher guard reported none, generic round1 opened with 30 launches. Runner env allowlist used a temporary HOME, CI, MOCK_EXTERNAL_APIS and the no-live-DB kill switch; no ordinary DATABASE_POOL_URL fallback was available. The worktree had only .env.example, so dotenv-swap recorded zero files. All baseline/mutant launches went through the runner supervisor with 120-second limits and shell:false argument arrays. Each semantic mutation was journaled before write, restored by matching the recorded mutant/original blob IDs after reaping, and marked restored. Root independently ran finalize after all 30 process groups were empty and confirmed tracked status clean and the original gate fingerprint current before these report bytes were created. No canonical report or source edit was made by report generation.

The deterministic checker path is `docs/reviews/red-team/codex-owner-operator-run-e-eb15ab/findings.{md,json}`. Current intake lists an unhashed path, so root requested these final report bytes be staged at `/private/tmp/run-e-round1-report/` until the routing mismatch is resolved. This report does not claim the repository sidecar check passes while canonical artifacts are absent.

## Authorizations

None. Generic round1 only. No extra budget, third round, accepted-risk finding, external delegate or provider call was authorized or used.
